# Third-Party License Texts

Generated by `python3 scripts/gen-third-party-notices.py`; do not edit. These are the license, copying, copyright and notice files
found anywhere in the source of every Rust package in the Linux build (see `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` for the inventory), the vendored
`iced_winit`, the fonts libcosmic embeds, and the Phosphor icons Signpost bundles. Identical texts appear once, followed by the packages that ship them.

## Packages Without Their Own License File

These packages ship no license file in their source. Their text is the upstream project's file at the revision Cargo recorded for the
release (`.cargo_vcs_info.json`, or the pinned git revision), fetched once into `licenses/overrides/`, except the entries under Provenance
Exceptions. Where the license is or includes Apache-2.0, BSL-1.0, CC0-1.0 or MPL-2.0, the standard text from another package is used instead.

| Package | License | Text |
|---|---|---|
| `cosmic-client-toolkit 0.2.0` | MIT | fetched from https://raw.githubusercontent.com/pop-os/cosmic-protocols/c0cff4db14c37ed954983158e4055aa94c7741d9/src/LICENSE |
| `cosmic-settings-config 1.9.0` | MPL-2.0 | standard MPL-2.0 text |
| `drm-fourcc 2.2.0` | MIT | fetched from https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dzfranklin/drm-fourcc-rs/bb1b81f184650e1418e1dce512e34071675206d7/LICENSE (exception, see below) |
| `fluent-langneg 0.13.1` | Apache-2.0 OR MIT | standard Apache-2.0 text |
| `gpu-descriptor 0.3.2` | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | standard Apache-2.0 text |
| `gpu-descriptor-types 0.2.0` | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | standard Apache-2.0 text |
| `harfrust 0.5.2` | MIT | fetched from https://raw.githubusercontent.com/harfbuzz/harfrust/efdae3142ab76a2f1524d72cff9e3dfdc5afd7ca/LICENSE |

| `lyon 1.0.19` | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | standard Apache-2.0 text |
| `lyon_algorithms 1.0.21` | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | standard Apache-2.0 text |
| `lyon_geom 1.0.19` | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | standard Apache-2.0 text |
| `lyon_path 1.0.19` | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | standard Apache-2.0 text |

| `profiling 1.0.18` | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | standard Apache-2.0 text |

## Provenance Exceptions

- `drm-fourcc 2.2.0`: Exception to the released-revision rule: the 2.2.0 release commit 7b9f80060ffd4b35cd236437e65a17fb517d163c (2021-09-05) has no LICENSE file. The Cargo manifest at 2.2.0 declares license = "MIT". This is the project's MIT license text from the later commit bb1b81f184650e1418e1dce512e34071675206d7 ("Add license text", 2023-07-23), the commit that first added a LICENSE file, 12 commits after the release.
